# Aesthetician - Preset Catalog

Generated by `scripts/gen_catalog.py`. Override any parameter with
`--set effect.param=value` (audio effects start with `a_`).
**426 presets · 680 variants.**

## ADJUST

### `basic-adjust` - Basic Adjustments (any)

The full correction toolkit on one panel: exposure, contrast, gamma, white balance, split tones, saturation, vibrance, hue and unsharp-mask sharpness.

**Variants:** `punchy` (Punchy - More contrast, saturation and edge definition.), `flat` (Flat - Lifted blacks and eased contrast, a log-ish starting point.), `warm-open` (Warm & Open - A third of a stop up with warmth and gentle vibrance.)

**Picture:**
- **Tone Curve** - (defaults)
- **Color Balance** - (defaults)
- **Saturation & Hue** - (defaults)
- **Sharpness** - (defaults)

*21 adjustable parameters in this preset.*

### `brightness-contrast` - Brightness & Contrast (any)

Tone controls alone: exposure in stops, contrast around a pivot, midtone gamma, black lift and a soft highlight shoulder.

**Variants:** `brighter` (Brighter - A third of a stop up with the highlights protected.), `darker` (Darker - A third of a stop down.), `punchy` (Punchy - Contrast up around a slightly low pivot.), `lifted` (Lifted - Low contrast with raised blacks.)

**Picture:**
- **Tone Curve** - (defaults)

*7 adjustable parameters in this preset.*

### `color-balance` - Color Balance (any)

White balance and color controls: warmth, tint, shadow and highlight split tones, saturation, vibrance and hue rotation.

**Variants:** `warmer` (Warmer - Toward orange, as shot in warmer light.), `cooler` (Cooler - Toward blue, as shot in cooler light.), `vivid` (Vivid - Saturation up with vibrance favoring the muted colors.), `muted` (Muted - Saturation eased off across the board.)

**Picture:**
- **Color Balance** - (defaults)
- **Saturation & Hue** - (defaults)

*11 adjustable parameters in this preset.*

### `sharpness` - Sharpness (any)

Unsharp-mask sharpening with an adjustable radius; negative amounts soften toward a plain blur instead.

**Variants:** `crisp` (Crisp - A moderate edge lift at a fine radius.), `very-crisp` (Very Crisp - A strong edge lift; watch for halos on hard lines.), `soften` (Soften - A gentle blend toward a wide blur.)

**Picture:**
- **Sharpness** - (defaults)

*3 adjustable parameters in this preset.*

## ARCHIVE

### `auth-atomic-age-industrial-futurism-1957` - Atomic-Age Industrial Futurism (1957)

A spotless corporate-future print with chrome machinery, turquoise-clean shadows, warm demonstration skin, precise Eastman separation, fine grain, and authoritative dynamic-microphone narration on polished magnetic mono.

**Variants:** `preservation-element` (Preservation Element - A carefully held source element with steadier registration, cleaner surfaces, and the same period response.), `circulation-copy` (Circulation Copy - A routine contemporary copy with another print or carrier generation, never a different edit.)

A narrow magnetic stripe on 16 mm release film: modest top end, mono head smear, stripe hiss, short oxide losses and the projector just behind the wall.

### `audio-church-pa-1972` - Church PA (1972)

A column speaker bolted to limestone: horn midrange arriving twice, three seconds of stone tail on every pause, the amplifier humming through the sermon.

### `auth-food-network-studio-2005` - Food-Network Studio Show (2005)

Warm tungsten-biased studio video with clean early-HD edges, gently polished highlights, restrained 4:2:0 chroma, interlaced delivery, and close lav audio under network compression.

### `cable-news-1991` - Cable News Desk (1991)

The 24-hour desk during the big story: clean studio cameras, a satellite window's smeared remote, AGC riding every anchor breath - urgency as a house style.

**Variants:** `tape-archive` (Aircheck Tape - The master control VHS that kept the receipts.), `live-shot` (Satellite Live Shot - The remote window takes over the frame.)

### `local-cable-infomercial-1997` - Local Cable Infomercial (1997)

A late-night local infomercial assembled on aging analog decks: clipped setup, fluorescent green drift, S-VHS-like edge ringing, composite crawl and an aggressively processed mono cable track.

**Variants:** `master-dub` (Master Dub - The station receives a clean dub with steadier time base, less crawl and a quieter Hi-Fi carrier.), `overnight-repeat` (Overnight Repeat - A reused station cassette adds green phase drift, dropouts and limiter pumping around every pause.), `source-clean` (Source-Clean Transfer - Preserves the period carrier and color response while disabling momentary source loss or masking.)

### `regional-weather-1973` - Regional Weather Studio (1973)

An early-color local weather segment from plumbicon cameras and a composite microwave feed: raised setup, cyan shadow drift, warm faces, smeared map colors and dry booth mono.

## CAPTIONS

### `cc-dtv-2004` - Digital TV Captions (2004)

A CEA-708 decoder with its factory defaults: clean mixed-case sans on a translucent smoked block, floating in the caption safe area of an early flat panel.

**Variants:** `mono` (Mono Face - The monospace font option buried in the settings menu.), `no-backing` (No Backing - Backing off, edges on, the other settings-menu path.)

### `cc-line21-1982` - Line-21 Closed Captions (1982)

A set-top decoder drawing Line-21 data: white monospace capitals in snug black cells, popped on whole and held. The caption look burned into every off-air recording.

**Variants:** `paint-on` (Paint-On - The decoder paints each caption in left to right.), `large` (Large Print - The big-print decoder mode for the back of the room.), `soft-cells` (Faded Cells - An older decoder whose background bars have gone thin.)

Realtime stenography rolling up the screen: three rows of capitals scrolling in black cells, a beat behind the anchor, the way live news captioning actually landed.

### `vcr-osd-1990` - VCR On-Screen Text (1990)

The deck's own character generator writing over playback: glowing dot-matrix capitals that sit wherever the firmware put them and shiver with the tape.

**Variants:** `green` (Counter Green - The green phosphor personality of another deck.), `orange` (Camcorder Amber - Amber lettering straight off a camcorder EVF.), `tracking` (Bad Tracking - A deck fighting the tape: heavy shiver.)

## DIGITAL

### `auth-anime-web-fansub-encode-2006` - Anime Web-Fansub Encode (2006)

An early x264 fansub carrier without added subtitles: oversharpened 4:2:0 line art, long-GOP low-bitrate H.264 mosquito texture, restrained chroma, and compact stereo AAC while every supplied frame remains in place.

**Variants:** `clean-master` (Clean Master - The best surviving generation: the same capture signature with the carrier held in spec.), `period-copy` (Period Copy - A routine contemporary copy: a little less bandwidth and a little more medium showing through.)

**Picture:**
- **Tone Curve** - `tone.contrast=1.06`, `tone.lift=0.012`, `tone.knee=0.86`

### `adventure-answer-print-1985` - Adventure Answer Print (1985)

Warm Kodak 35mm under practical lamps: blue-gray shadow detail, damp green exteriors, flare in the haze and a well-traveled answer print with warm, slightly compressed theatrical sound.

## MODERN

### `auth-asmr-close-mic-2018` - ASMR Close-Mic Video (2018)

A clean H.264 mirrorless close-up with gentle highlight rolloff and neutral color, paired with wide-band high-rate AAC so whispers, breath, and tiny transients remain physically close.

**Variants:** `clean-master` (Clean Master - The best surviving generation: the same capture signature with the carrier held in spec.), `period-copy` (Period Copy - A routine contemporary copy: a little less bandwidth and a little more medium showing through.)

**Picture:**
- **Tone Curve** - `tone.contrast=0.98`, `tone.lift=0.008`, `tone.knee=0.9`
- **Color Balance** - `balance.warmth=0.04`, `balance.tint=0.0`

### `cband-superstation-1982` - C-Band Superstation Feed (1982)

A cable-headend recording of a C-band superstation: soft tube-origin color, satellite fade, co-channel ghosts, composite ringing, interlace and a narrowed mono subcarrier passed through two analog generations.

**Variants:** `clear-sky` (Clear-Sky Feed - The downlink is well peaked, leaving only the composite decoder and mild headend generation loss.), `rain-fade` (Rain Fade - A marginal downlink breathes in noise, loses color density and admits a second station underneath.)

### `jammed-broadcast-1984` - Jammed Channel (1984)

### `auth-cinerama-travel-spectacle-1958` - Cinerama Travel Spectacle (1958)

A premium multi-panel travel presentation translated without cropping or drawing seams: brilliant Eastman vistas, faint layer-registration divergence, exceptionally fine grain, steady density, and broad seven-channel-era magnetic character.

**Variants:** `preservation-element` (Preservation Element - A carefully held source element with steadier registration, cleaner surfaces, and the same period response.), `circulation-copy` (Circulation Copy - A routine contemporary copy with another print or carrier generation, never a different edit.)

### `supermarionation-print-1966` - Supermarionation Print (1966)

A bright British miniature-stage print: dense primary dyes, crisp model highlights, shallow-scale haze, controlled optical joins and clean compressed mono sound.

**Variants:** `effects-reel` (Effects Reel - Optical generations soften the model edges and show brighter matte seams through the haze.), `studio-master` (Studio Master - The protected element has finer grain, clean joins and almost no gate movement.)

**Picture:**
- **Stock / Process Look** - `stock.profile=eastman_60s`, `stock.strength=0.82`

### `tokyo-spectacle-1962` - Tokyo Spectacle Print (1962)

Tokyo-studio Eastmancolor: cyan sky, emerald sets and crimson costumes printed through soft optical mattes, then worn into a magenta-leaning television survivor.

**Variants:** `miniature-stage` (Miniature Stage - The composite-heavy effects reel: softer model depth, brighter matte seams and heavier print breathing.), `swordplay-stage` (Swordplay Stage - Cleaner mattes, amber tungsten and a slightly roomier optical mono track for the studio duel.), `television-transfer` (Television Transfer - The 4:3 station element: softer bandwidth, stronger hue drift and another decade in the can.)
